,
we obtain the following explicit numerical bounds.

Proposition 6.2
.

Let
\alpha,\beta\geq-1/2
be such that
\alpha+\beta+1\in\mathbb{N}\setminus\{0\}
. Then

\displaystyle c_{\alpha,\beta,T}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{11\cdot(18/5)^{\alpha+\beta+1}}\qquad\textrm{for}\;\;T>0,

\displaystyle C_{\alpha,\beta,T}

\displaystyle\leq\begin{cases}(11/3)\cdot(17/5)^{\alpha+\beta+1}&\;\;\textrm{if}\;\;T=2/(\alpha+\beta+3/2)\\
16\cdot(8/5)^{\alpha+\beta+1}&\;\;\textrm{if}\;\;T=1/(\alpha+\beta+3/2)^{2}\end{cases},

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\alpha,\beta,T}}{c_{\alpha,\beta,T}}

\displaystyle\leq\begin{cases}40\cdot 12^{\alpha+\beta+1}&\;\;\textrm{if}\;\;T=2/(\alpha+\beta+3/2)\\
172\cdot(28/5)^{\alpha+\beta+1}&\;\;\textrm{if}\;\;T=1/(\alpha+\beta+3/2)^{2}\end{cases}.

The case when

\mathbf{\Lambda\in\mathbb{N}}
.

By Lemmas
F
and
B
we have

\displaystyle c_{\alpha,\beta,T}

\displaystyle=c^{B}_{\Lambda,T/4}\,{4^{\Lambda+1}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta}}\,\frac{h_{0}^{\Lambda}}{h_{0}^{\alpha,\beta}}=c^{A}_{2\Lambda+1/2,T/16}\,\frac{4^{3\Lambda+5/2}\,b_{\Lambda}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta}}{\big(\mathbb{D}_{\Lambda}\,T/4\vee\mathfrak{B}\pi^{2}\big)^{\Lambda+1/2}}\,\frac{h_{0}^{2\Lambda+1/2}}{h_{0}^{\alpha,\beta}},

\displaystyle C_{\alpha,\beta,T}

\displaystyle=C^{B}_{\Lambda,T/4}\,{4^{\Lambda+2}B_{\alpha}B_{\beta}}\,\frac{h_{0}^{\Lambda}}{h_{0}^{\alpha,\beta}}=C^{A}_{2\Lambda+1/2,T/16}\,4^{3\Lambda+4}\,B_{\Lambda}B_{\alpha}B_{\beta}\,\frac{h_{0}^{2\Lambda+1/2}}{h_{0}^{\alpha,\beta}}.

Since
\mathbb{D}_{\Lambda}T/4\leq\mathfrak{B}\pi^{2}
for
T/16\leq 1/(4\Lambda+3)
, we have (see also (
6.5
) and (
2.6
))

\displaystyle c_{\alpha,\beta,T}

\displaystyle=c^{A}_{2\Lambda+1/2,T/16}\,\frac{4^{3\Lambda+5/2}\,(1\wedge 2^{\Lambda-1/2})(1\wedge 2^{\alpha-1/2})(1\wedge 2^{\beta-1/2})\Gamma(\Lambda+1)\Gamma(\alpha+1)\Gamma(\beta+1)}{\pi^{3/2}(\mathfrak{B}\pi^{2})^{\Lambda+1/2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times e^{-\mathbb{D}_{\Lambda}-\chi_{\{\alpha\neq-1/2\}}\mathbb{D}_{\alpha}-\chi_{\{\beta\neq-1/2\}}\mathbb{D}_{\beta}}\,\frac{h_{0}^{2\Lambda+1/2}}{h_{0}^{\alpha,\beta}}

\displaystyle=c^{A}_{2\Lambda+1/2,T/16}\,\frac{4^{3\Lambda+5/2}\,(1\wedge 2^{\Lambda-1/2})(1\wedge 2^{\alpha-1/2})(1\wedge 2^{\beta-1/2})\Gamma(2\Lambda+3/2)2^{-3\Lambda-3/2}}{\sqrt{\pi}(\mathfrak{B}\pi^{2})^{\Lambda+1/2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times e^{-\mathbb{D}_{\Lambda}-\chi_{\{\alpha\neq-1/2\}}\mathbb{D}_{\alpha}-\chi_{\{\beta\neq-1/2\}}\mathbb{D}_{\beta}}

\displaystyle\geq c^{A}_{2\Lambda+1/2,T/16}\,\frac{2^{4\Lambda+3/2}\Gamma(2\Lambda+3/2)}{\pi^{2\Lambda+3/2}}\,\exp\Big(-\frac{2\Lambda+4}{e^{\gamma}}\Big),

\displaystyle C_{\alpha,\beta,T}

\displaystyle=C^{A}_{2\Lambda+1/2,T/16}\,4^{3\Lambda+4}\,(1\vee 2^{\Lambda-1/2})(1\vee 2^{\alpha-1/2})(1\vee 2^{\beta-1/2})\,2^{-\chi_{\{\alpha=-1/2\}}-\chi_{\{\beta=-1/2\}}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\frac{\Gamma(\Lambda+1)\Gamma(\alpha+1)\Gamma(\beta+1)}{\pi^{3/2}}\,\frac{h_{0}^{2\Lambda+1/2}}{h_{0}^{\alpha,\beta}}

\displaystyle=C^{A}_{2\Lambda+1/2,T/16}\,4^{3\Lambda+4}\,(1\vee 2^{\Lambda-1/2})(1\vee 2^{\alpha-1/2})(1\vee 2^{\beta-1/2})\,2^{-\chi_{\{\alpha=-1/2\}}-\chi_{\{\beta=-1/2\}}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\Gamma(2\Lambda+3/2)\,2^{-3\Lambda-3/2}/\sqrt{\pi}

\displaystyle\leq C^{A}_{2\Lambda+1/2,T/16}\,2^{5\Lambda+7}\,\Gamma(2\Lambda+3/2)/\sqrt{\pi},

provided that
T/16\leq 1/(4\Lambda+3)
. Further, we have

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\alpha,\beta,T}}{c_{\alpha,\beta,T}}\leq\frac{C^{A}_{2\Lambda+1/2,T/16}}{c^{A}_{2\Lambda+1/2,T/16}}\,2^{\Lambda+11/2}\pi^{2\Lambda+1}\,\exp\Big(\frac{2\Lambda+4}{e^{\gamma}}\Big),

provided that
T/16\leq 1/(4\Lambda+3)
.

Summarizing, we obtain

\displaystyle c_{\alpha,\beta,T}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}\pi^{2\Lambda+3/2}}\,\exp\Big(-\frac{2\Lambda+4}{e^{\gamma}}\Big)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}\exp(3e^{-\gamma})\,[\pi^{2}\exp(2e^{-\gamma})]^{\Lambda+1/2}}\qquad\textrm{if}\;\;T=16/(4\Lambda+3),

\displaystyle C_{\alpha,\beta,T}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2^{5\Lambda+7}}{\sqrt{\pi}}\times\begin{cases}\mathfrak{w}_{0}(\sqrt{e}\,\mathfrak{w}_{1}/2)^{2\Lambda+1}&\;\;\textrm{if}\;\;T=16/(4\Lambda+3)\\
\sqrt[4]{e}\,\mathfrak{w}^{\prime}_{0}(\pi/8)^{2\Lambda+1}&\;\;\textrm{if}\;\;T=16/(4\Lambda+3)^{2}\end{cases}

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}16\sqrt{2}\,\pi^{-1/2}\,\mathfrak{w}_{0}\,[8e\,\mathfrak{w}_{1}^{2}]^{\Lambda+1/2}&\;\;\textrm{if}\;\;T=16/(4\Lambda+3)\\
16\sqrt{2}\,\pi^{-1/2}\,\sqrt[4]{e}\,\mathfrak{w}^{\prime}_{0}\,[\pi^{2}/2]^{\Lambda+1/2}&\;\;\textrm{if}\;\;T=16/(4\Lambda+3)^{2}\end{cases},

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\alpha,\beta,T}}{c_{\alpha,\beta,T}}

\displaystyle\leq 2^{5\Lambda+15/2}\pi^{2\Lambda+1}\,\exp\Big(\frac{2\Lambda+4}{e^{\gamma}}\Big)\times\begin{cases}\mathfrak{w}_{0}(\sqrt{e}\,\mathfrak{w}_{1}/2)^{2\Lambda+1}&\;\;\textrm{if}\;\;T=16/(4\Lambda+3)\\
\sqrt[4]{e}\,\mathfrak{w}^{\prime}_{0}(\pi/8)^{2\Lambda+1}&\;\;\textrm{if}\;\;T=16/(4\Lambda+3)^{2}\end{cases}

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}32\,\mathfrak{w}_{0}\exp(3e^{-\gamma})\,[8\pi^{2}\mathfrak{w}_{1}^{2}\exp(2e^{-\gamma}+1)]^{\Lambda+1/2}&\;\;\textrm{if}\;\;T=16/(4\Lambda+3)\\
32\,\mathfrak{w}^{\prime}_{0}\exp(3e^{-\gamma}+1/4)\,[2^{-1}\pi^{4}\exp(2e^{-\gamma})]^{\Lambda+1/2}&\;\;\textrm{if}\;\;T=16/(4\Lambda+3)^{2}\end{cases}.

Approximating above the relevant expressions
34
34

34

One has

\sqrt{2\pi}\exp(3e^{-\gamma})\approx 13.508471
,

\pi^{2}\exp(2e^{-\gamma})\approx 30.337323
,

,

,

,

,
we get the following numerical bounds.

Proposition 6.3
.

Let
\alpha,\beta\geq-1/2
be such that
\alpha+\beta+1/2\in\mathbb{N}
. Then

\displaystyle c_{\alpha,\beta,T}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{14\cdot 31^{\alpha+\beta+1}}\qquad\textrm{if}\;\;T=4/(\alpha+\beta+5/4),

\displaystyle C_{\alpha,\beta,T}

\displaystyle\leq\begin{cases}11\cdot 23^{\alpha+\beta+1}&\;\;\textrm{if}\;\;T=4/(\alpha+\beta+5/4)\\
45\cdot 5^{\alpha+\beta+1}&\;\;\textrm{if}\;\;T=1/(\alpha+\beta+5/4)^{2}\end{cases},

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\alpha,\beta,T}}{c_{\alpha,\beta,T}}

\displaystyle\leq\begin{cases}138\cdot 694^{\alpha+\beta+1}&\;\;\textrm{if}\;\;T=4/(\alpha+\beta+5/4)\\
603\cdot 150^{\alpha+\beta+1}&\;\;\textrm{if}\;\;T=1/(\alpha+\beta+5/4)^{2}\end{cases}.

The case when

\mathbf{\Lambda\in(0,\infty)\setminus(\mathbb{N}/2)}
.

We have, see Lemmas
F
,
C
,
B
,

\displaystyle c_{\alpha,\beta,T}

\displaystyle=c^{C}_{\Lambda,T/4}\,{4^{\Lambda+1}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta}}\,\frac{h_{0}^{\Lambda}}{h_{0}^{\alpha,\beta}}

\displaystyle=c^{A}_{\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}+1/2,T/16}\,\frac{4^{2\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}+5/2}\,b_{\breve{\Lambda}}\,b_{\alpha}b_{\beta}}{\big(\mathbb{D}_{\breve{\Lambda}}\,T/4\vee\mathfrak{B}\pi^{2}\big)^{\breve{\Lambda}+1/2}}\,\frac{h_{0}^{\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}+1/2}\,h_{0}^{\Lambda}}{h_{0}^{\Lambda,\breve{\Lambda}}\,h_{0}^{\alpha,\beta}}\,\omega_{\Lambda}(T/4),

\displaystyle C_{\alpha,\beta,T}

\displaystyle=C^{C}_{\Lambda,T/4}\,{4^{\Lambda+2}B_{\alpha}B_{\beta}}\,\frac{h_{0}^{\Lambda}}{h_{0}^{\alpha,\beta}}

\displaystyle=C^{A}_{\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}-1/2,T/16}\,4^{2\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}+3}\,B_{\breve{\Lambda}-1}\,B_{\alpha}B_{\beta}\,\frac{h_{0}^{\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}-1/2}\,h_{0}^{\Lambda}}{h_{0}^{\Lambda,\breve{\Lambda}-1}\,h_{0}^{\alpha,\beta}}\,\Omega_{\Lambda}(T/4).

Notice that
\breve{\Lambda}-1\neq-1/2
.
Taking into account that
\mathbb{D}_{\breve{\Lambda}}T/4\leq\mathfrak{B}\pi^{2}
for

T/16\leq 1/(2\Lambda+2\breve{\Lambda}+3)
,
and using the bounds
(1\wedge 2^{\alpha-1/2})(1\wedge 2^{\beta-1/2})\geq 2^{-3/2}
,

(1\vee 2^{\alpha-1/2})(1\vee 2^{\beta-1/2})\leq 2^{\Lambda}
and also (
6.5
) and (
2.6
), we obtain

\displaystyle c_{\alpha,\beta,T}

\displaystyle=c^{A}_{\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}+1/2,T/16}\,\frac{4^{2\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}+5/2}\,b_{\breve{\Lambda}}\,b_{\alpha}b_{\beta}}{(\mathfrak{B}\pi^{2})^{\breve{\Lambda}+1/2}}\,\frac{h_{0}^{\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}+1/2}\,h_{0}^{\Lambda}}{h_{0}^{\Lambda,\breve{\Lambda}}\,h_{0}^{\alpha,\beta}}\,\omega_{\Lambda}(T/4)

\displaystyle=c^{A}_{\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}+1/2,T/16}\,\frac{4^{2\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}+5/2}\,(1\wedge 2^{\breve{\Lambda}-1/2})(1\wedge 2^{\alpha-1/2})(1\wedge 2^{\beta-1/2})\Gamma(\breve{\Lambda}+1)\Gamma(\alpha+1)\Gamma(\beta+1)}{\pi^{3/2}(\mathfrak{B}\pi^{2})^{\breve{\Lambda}+1/2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times e^{-\mathbb{D}_{\breve{\Lambda}}-\chi_{\{\alpha\neq-1/2\}}\mathbb{D}_{\alpha}-\chi_{\{\beta\neq-1/2\}}\mathbb{D}_{\beta}}\,\frac{h_{0}^{\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}+1/2}\,h_{0}^{\Lambda}}{h_{0}^{\Lambda,\breve{\Lambda}}\,h_{0}^{\alpha,\beta}}\,\omega_{\Lambda}(T/4)

\displaystyle=c^{A}_{\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}+1/2,T/16}\,\frac{2^{2\Lambda+2\breve{\Lambda}+4}\,(1\wedge 2^{\breve{\Lambda}-1/2})(1\wedge 2^{\alpha-1/2})(1\wedge 2^{\beta-1/2})}{\pi^{2\breve{\Lambda}+3/2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times e^{-\mathbb{D}_{\breve{\Lambda}}-\chi_{\{\alpha\neq-1/2\}}\mathbb{D}_{\alpha}-\chi_{\{\beta\neq-1/2\}}\mathbb{D}_{\beta}}\,\Gamma(\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}+3/2)\,\omega_{\Lambda}(T/4)

\displaystyle\geq c^{A}_{\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}+1/2,T/16}\,\frac{2^{2\Lambda+2\breve{\Lambda}+5/2}\Gamma(\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}+3/2)}{\pi^{2\breve{\Lambda}+3/2}}\,\exp\Big(-\frac{\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}+4}{e^{\gamma}}\Big)\,e^{-(\Lambda+3/4)T/4},

\displaystyle C_{\alpha,\beta,T}

\displaystyle=C^{A}_{\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}-1/2,T/16}\,4^{2\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}+3}\,B_{\breve{\Lambda}-1}\,B_{\alpha}B_{\beta}\,\frac{h_{0}^{\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}-1/2}\,h_{0}^{\Lambda}}{h_{0}^{\Lambda,\breve{\Lambda}-1}\,h_{0}^{\alpha,\beta}}\,\Omega_{\Lambda}(T/4)

\displaystyle=C^{A}_{\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}-1/2,T/16}\,4^{2\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}+3}\,\frac{(1\vee 2^{\breve{\Lambda}-3/2})(1\vee 2^{\alpha-1/2})(1\vee 2^{\beta-1/2})\,2^{-\chi_{\{\alpha=-1/2\}}-\chi_{\{\beta=-1/2\}}}}{\pi^{3/2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\Gamma(\breve{\Lambda})\Gamma(\alpha+1)\Gamma(\beta+1)\,\frac{h_{0}^{\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}-1/2}\,h_{0}^{\Lambda}}{h_{0}^{\Lambda,\breve{\Lambda}-1}\,h_{0}^{\alpha,\beta}}\,\Omega_{\Lambda}(T/4)

\displaystyle=C^{A}_{\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}-1/2,T/16}\,4^{2\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}+3}\,\frac{(1\vee 2^{\breve{\Lambda}-3/2})(1\vee 2^{\alpha-1/2})(1\vee 2^{\beta-1/2})\,2^{-\chi_{\{\alpha=-1/2\}}-\chi_{\{\beta=-1/2\}}}}{\sqrt{\pi}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\frac{\Gamma(\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}+1/2)}{2^{2\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}+1/2}}\,\Omega_{\Lambda}(T/4)

\displaystyle\leq C^{A}_{\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}-1/2,T/16}\,2^{3\Lambda+2\breve{\Lambda}+5}\pi^{-1/2}\,\Gamma(\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}+1/2)\,e^{(\Lambda+1/2)T/4},

provided that
T/16\leq 1/(2\Lambda+2\breve{\Lambda}+3)
.
Further, we have

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\alpha,\beta,T}}{c_{\alpha,\beta,T}}

\displaystyle\leq 2^{\Lambda+5/2}\pi^{2\breve{\Lambda}+1}\,\frac{C^{A}_{\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}-1/2,T/16}}{c^{A}_{\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}+1/2,T/16}\,(\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}+1/2)}\,e^{(2\Lambda+5/4)T/4}\,\exp\Big(\frac{\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}+4}{e^{\gamma}}\Big),

provided that
T/16\leq 1/(2\Lambda+2\breve{\Lambda}+3)
.

Summarizing, since
\breve{\Lambda}\in[\Lambda,\Lambda+1)
, we obtain

\displaystyle c_{\alpha,\beta,T}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\sqrt{2}}{\pi^{2\Lambda+7/2}}\exp\Big(-\frac{2\Lambda+5}{e^{\gamma}}\Big)\,e^{-1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2^{-1/2}\pi^{5/2}\exp(4e^{-\gamma}+1)\,[\pi^{2}\exp(2e^{-\gamma})]^{\Lambda+1/2}}\qquad\textrm{if}\;\;T=16/(4\Lambda+5),

\displaystyle C_{\alpha,\beta,T}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2^{5\Lambda+7}}{\sqrt{\pi}}\times\begin{cases}e\,\mathfrak{w}_{0}(\sqrt{e}\,\mathfrak{w}_{1}/2)^{2\Lambda+1}&\;\;\textrm{if}\;\;T=16/(4\Lambda+5)\\
e^{1/3}\,\mathfrak{w}^{\prime}_{0}(\pi/8)^{2\Lambda}&\;\;\textrm{if}\;\;T=16/(4\Lambda+5)^{2}\end{cases}

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}16\sqrt{2}\pi^{-1/2}e\,\mathfrak{w}_{0}\,[8e\,\mathfrak{w}_{1}^{2}]^{\Lambda+1/2}&\;\;\textrm{if}\;\;T=16/(4\Lambda+5)\\
128\sqrt{2}\pi^{-3/2}e^{1/3}\,\mathfrak{w}^{\prime}_{0}\,[\pi^{2}/2]^{\Lambda+1/2}&\;\;\textrm{if}\;\;T=16/(4\Lambda+5)^{2}\end{cases},

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\alpha,\beta,T}}{c_{\alpha,\beta,T}}

\displaystyle\leq 2^{5\Lambda+13/2}\pi^{2\Lambda+3}\,\exp\Big(\frac{2\Lambda+5}{e^{\gamma}}\Big)\times\begin{cases}e^{2}\,\mathfrak{w}_{0}(\sqrt{e}\,\mathfrak{w}_{1}/2)^{2\Lambda+1}&\;\;\textrm{if}\;\;T=16/(4\Lambda+5)\\
e^{1/5}\,\sqrt[4]{e}\,\mathfrak{w}^{\prime}_{0}(\pi/8)^{2\Lambda}&\;\;\textrm{if}\;\;T=16/(4\Lambda+5)^{2}\end{cases}

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}16\pi^{2}\exp(4e^{-\gamma}+2)\,\mathfrak{w}_{0}\,[8\pi^{2}\exp(2e^{-\gamma}+1)\,\mathfrak{w}_{1}^{2}]^{\Lambda+1/2}&\;\;\textrm{if}\;\;T=16/(4\Lambda+5)\\
128\pi\,\mathfrak{w}^{\prime}_{0}\,\exp(4e^{-\gamma}+9/20)\,[2^{-1}\pi^{4}\exp(2e^{-\gamma})]^{\Lambda+1/2}&\;\;\textrm{if}\;\;T=16/(4\Lambda+5)^{2}\end{cases}.

Approximating above the relevant expressions
35
35

35

One has

2^{-1/2}\pi^{5/2}\exp(4e^{-\gamma}+1)\approx 317.694128
,

\pi^{2}\exp(2e^{-\gamma})\approx 30.337323
,

16\sqrt{2}\pi^{-1/2}e\,\mathfrak{w}_{0}\approx 27.691605
,

8e\,\mathfrak{w}_{1}^{2}\approx 22.872438
,

,

\pi^{2}/2\approx 4.934802
,

16\pi^{2}\exp(4e^{-\gamma}+2)\,\mathfrak{w}_{0}\ \approx 8797.460403
,

,

,

.

,
we obtain the following explicit numerical bounds.

Proposition 6.4
.

Let
\alpha,\beta\geq-1/2
be such that
\alpha+\beta+1/2>0
and
2\alpha+2\beta+1\notin\mathbb{N}
. Then

\displaystyle c_{\alpha,\beta,T}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{318\cdot 31^{\alpha+\beta+1}}\qquad\textrm{if}\;\;T=4/(\alpha+\beta+7/4),

\displaystyle C_{\alpha,\beta,T}

\displaystyle\leq\begin{cases}28\cdot 23^{\alpha+\beta+1}&\;\;\textrm{if}\;\;T=4/(\alpha+\beta+7/4)\\
124\cdot 5^{\alpha+\beta+1}&\;\;\textrm{if}\;\;T=1/(\alpha+\beta+7/4)^{2}\end{cases},

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\alpha,\beta,T}}{c_{\alpha,\beta,T}}

\displaystyle\leq\begin{cases}8798\cdot 694^{\alpha+\beta+1}&\;\;\textrm{if}\;\;T=4/(\alpha+\beta+7/4)\\
16214\cdot 150^{\alpha+\beta+1}&\;\;\textrm{if}\;\;T=1/(\alpha+\beta+7/4)^{2}\end{cases}.

The case when

\mathbf{\Lambda\in(-1/2,0)}
.

From Lemma
F
we have

c_{\alpha,\beta,T}=c^{D}_{\Lambda,T/4}\,{4^{\Lambda+1}b_{\alpha}b_{\beta}}\,\frac{h_{0}^{\Lambda}}{h_{0}^{\alpha,\beta}},\qquad C_{\alpha,\beta,T}=C^{D}_{\Lambda,T/4}\,{4^{\Lambda+2}B_{\alpha}B_{\beta}}\,\frac{h_{0}^{\Lambda}}{h_{0}^{\alpha,\beta}}.

Using (
6.12
), (
6.5
) and the fact that

\displaystyle\mathbb{D}_{\breve{\lambda}+2}T/4\leq\mathfrak{B}\pi^{2}\qquad\text{when \; $\lambda>-1$ \; and \; $T/16\leq 1/(2\Lambda+2\breve{\Lambda}+11)$,}

we get (see also (
2.6
) and (
4.1
))

\displaystyle c_{\alpha,\beta,T}

\displaystyle=\frac{2^{3\Lambda+2\breve{\Lambda}+29/2}}{\pi^{2}}\,e^{-(4\Lambda+6)T/4}\,c^{A}_{\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}+9/2,T/16}\,\pi^{-2\breve{\Lambda}-11/2}\,\frac{\Gamma(\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}+11/2)}{\Lambda+2}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\omega_{\Lambda+2}(T/4)\,\frac{1-2^{-\Lambda-2}}{e}\,e^{-\mathbb{D}_{\breve{\Lambda}+2}-\chi_{\{\alpha\neq-1/2\}}\mathbb{D}_{\alpha}-\chi_{\{\beta\neq-1/2\}}\mathbb{D}_{\beta}}

\displaystyle\geq 2^{3\Lambda+2\breve{\Lambda}+29/2}\,\pi^{-2\breve{\Lambda}-15/2}\,e^{-(5\Lambda+35/4)T/4}\,c^{A}_{\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}+9/2,T/16}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\frac{\Gamma(\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}+11/2)}{\Lambda+2}\,\frac{1-2^{-\Lambda-2}}{e}\,\exp\Big(-\frac{\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}+6}{e^{\gamma}}\Big),

\displaystyle C_{\alpha,\beta,T}

\displaystyle=\frac{2^{2\Lambda+2\breve{\Lambda}+17}}{\pi^{5/2}}\,C^{A}_{\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}+7/2,T/16}\,\frac{\Gamma(\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}+9/2)}{\Lambda+2}\,\Omega_{\Lambda+2}(T/4)

\displaystyle\qquad\times\frac{1}{(1-1/e)^{2}}\,\Big(\frac{4T}{\pi^{2}}+1\Big)^{\Lambda+1}\,(1\vee 2^{\alpha-1/2})(1\vee 2^{\beta-1/2})\,2^{-\chi_{\{\alpha=-1/2\}}-\chi_{\{\beta=-1/2\}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2^{2\Lambda+2\breve{\Lambda}+17}}{\pi^{5/2}}\,C^{A}_{\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}+7/2,T/16}\,\frac{\Gamma(\Lambda+\breve{\Lambda}+9/2)}{\Lambda+2}\,e^{(\Lambda+5/2)T/4}\,\frac{1}{(1-1/e)^{2}}\,\Big(\frac{4T}{\pi^{2}}+1\Big)^{\Lambda+1},

provided that
T/16\leq 1/(2\Lambda+2\breve{\Lambda}+11)
.
Since
\Lambda\in(-1/2,0)
, one has
\breve{\Lambda}=-\Lambda
and consequently

c_{\alpha,\beta,T}\geq\frac{2^{13}}{\pi^{17/2}}\,e^{-35T/16}\,c^{A}_{9/2,T/16}\,\Gamma(11/2)\,{\frac{1}{2e}}\exp\Big(-\frac{6}{e^{\gamma}}\Big)

and

C_{\alpha,\beta,T}\leq\frac{2^{18}}{3\pi^{5/2}}\,C^{A}_{7/2,T/16}\,\Gamma(9/2)\,e^{5T/8}\,\frac{1}{(1-1/e)^{2}}\,\Big(\frac{4T}{\pi^{2}}+1\Big)^{\Lambda+1},

provided that
T/16\leq 1/11
.
Furthermore, using once again the above identities for
c_{\alpha,\beta,T}
and
C_{\alpha,\beta,T}
, and also (
2.6
), we get

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\alpha,\beta,T}}{c_{\alpha,\beta,T}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2^{4}\pi^{6}}{9}\,\frac{C^{A}_{7/2,T/16}}{c^{A}_{9/2,T/16}}\,e^{45T/16}\,\frac{1}{(1-1/e)^{2}}\,\Big(\frac{4T}{\pi^{2}}+1\Big)^{\Lambda+1}\frac{e}{1-2^{-\Lambda-2}}\exp\Big(\frac{6}{e^{\gamma}}\Big),

provided that
T/16\leq 1/11
.

Summarizing, we obtain

\displaystyle c_{\alpha,\beta,T}

\displaystyle\geq 4\,\pi^{-17/2}\,e^{-46/11}\,\exp\Big(-\frac{6}{e^{\gamma}}\Big)\qquad\textrm{if}\;\;T=16/11,

\displaystyle C_{\alpha,\beta,T}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2^{18}}{3\pi^{5/2}}\,\frac{1}{(1-1/e)^{2}}\,\Big(\frac{4T}{\pi^{2}}+1\Big)\times\begin{cases}e^{10/11}\,\mathfrak{w}_{0}(\sqrt{e}\,\mathfrak{w}_{1}/2)^{4}&\;\;\textrm{if}\;\;T=16/11\\
e^{161/484}\,\mathfrak{w}^{\prime}_{0}(\pi/8)^{4}&\;\;\textrm{if}\;\;T=16/121\end{cases},

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\alpha,\beta,T}}{c_{\alpha,\beta,T}}

\displaystyle\leq 2^{13}\,\pi^{6}\,\frac{(1-2^{-3/2})^{-1}}{(1-1/e)^{2}}\,\Big(\frac{4T}{\pi^{2}}+1\Big)\exp\Big(\frac{6}{e^{\gamma}}\Big)\times\begin{cases}e^{56/11}\,\mathfrak{w}_{0}(\sqrt{e}\,\mathfrak{w}_{1}/2)^{4}&\;\;\textrm{if}\;\;T=16/11\\
e^{785/484}\,\mathfrak{w}^{\prime}_{0}(\pi/8)^{4}&\;\;\textrm{if}\;\;T=16/121\end{cases}.

Approximating above the relevant expressions
36
36

36

One has

2^{-2}\pi^{17/2}\exp(6e^{-\gamma}+46/11)\approx 7.996262\cdot 10^{6}
,

\frac{2^{14}}{3\pi^{5/2}}\big(\frac{64}{11\pi^{2}}+1\big)\frac{e^{32/11}}{(1-1/e)^{2}}\mathfrak{w}_{0}\mathfrak{w}_{1}^{4}\approx 20106.561157
,

\frac{64\pi^{3/2}}{3}\big(\frac{64}{121\pi^{2}}+1\big)\frac{e^{161/484}}{(1-1/e)^{2}}\mathfrak{w}^{\prime}_{0}\approx 1188.639581
,

2^{9}\pi^{6}\big(\frac{1}{1-2^{-3/2}}\big)\big(\frac{e}{1-1/e}\big)^{2}\big(\frac{64}{11\pi^{2}}+1\big)\exp(6e^{-\gamma}+56/11)\mathfrak{w}_{0}\mathfrak{w}_{1}^{4}\approx 9.326602\cdot 10^{10}
,

2\pi^{10}\big(\frac{1}{1-2^{-3/2}}\big)\frac{1}{(1-1/e)^{2}}\big(\frac{64}{121\pi^{2}}+1\big)\exp(6e^{-\gamma}+785/484)\mathfrak{w}^{\prime}_{0}\approx 3.056413\cdot 10^{8}
.

we get the following explicit numerical bounds.

Proposition 6.5
.

Let
\alpha,\beta\geq-1/2
be such that
\alpha+\beta+1/2\in(-1/2,0)
. Then

\displaystyle c_{\alpha,\beta,T}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{8\cdot 10^{6}}\qquad\textrm{if}\;\;T=16/11,

\displaystyle C_{\alpha,\beta,T}

\displaystyle\leq\begin{cases}20107&\;\;\textrm{if}\;\;T=16/11\\
1189&\;\;\textrm{if}\;\;T=16/121\end{cases},

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\alpha,\beta,T}}{c_{\alpha,\beta,T}}

\displaystyle\leq\begin{cases}(28/3)\cdot 10^{10}&\;\;\textrm{if}\;\;T=16/11\\
31\cdot 10^{7}&\;\;\textrm{if}\;\;T=16/121\end{cases}.

6.4.
Comments on other related heat kernels

The results of Sections
4
and
5
can also be used to obtain totally explicit sharp bounds
for further heat kernels that are related to the Jacobi heat kernel. We will not pursue this matter here, however,
we indicate for interested readers some of the heat kernels in question.

Fourier–Bessel and Fourier–Dini heat kernels

Sharp bounds for the Fourier–Bessel heat kernel were proved in
[
21
]
by a combination of probabilistic and analytic
methods. Earlier, qualitatively sharp estimates were obtained in
[
23
,
24
]
.
A relation with the Jacobi heat kernel
37
37

37

The Jacobi heat kernel used in
[
24
]

is not exactly
G_{t}^{\alpha,\beta}(x,y)
, nevertheless both kernels are interrelated by a simple identity.

established in
[
24
]
, see
[
24
, Rem. 3.3]
, delivers a fully analytic proof of the sharp heat kernel bounds and,
together with the results of this paper, explicit multiplicative constants.

Sharp estimates for a certain Fourier–Dini heat kernel were proved recently in
[
20
]
. Similarly as in the Fourier–Bessel case,
there exists a connection with the Jacobi heat kernel, see
[
20
, Prop. 3.6]
, which together with our present results
leads to explicit multiplicative constants in the Fourier–Dini heat kernel bounds.

Heat kernels related to balls and simplices

Sharp estimates for heat kernels corresponding to certain orthogonal expansions in multi-dimensional balls and simplices were
obtained in
[
28
]
. Those kernels are related to the Jacobi heat kernel via certain integral formulas, see

[
28
, Sec. 5,6]
, which, together with the present results, can be used to find explicit multiplicative constants
in the bounds in question.
Prior to these results, qualitatively sharp estimates for the discussed heat kernels had been proved independently
in
[
37
]
and
[
18
,
19
]
.

Heat kernels related to cones and conical surfaces

Sharp bounds for heat kernels associated with orthogonal expansions in multi-dimensional cones and conical surfaces
were derived in
[
14
]
. Again, there is a connection with the Jacobi heat kernel via integral formulas
(see
[
14
, Lem. 2.1, Lem. 2.2]
) which enables
one to find, with the aid of the present paper, explicit multiplicative constants in the heat kernel estimates.

Heat kernels related to other solids and surfaces of revolution

There are other domains admitting orthogonal expansions for which the related heat kernels are potentially
related with the Jacobi heat kernel, e.g. multi-dimensional paraboloids and hyperboloids, and their surfaces, cf.
[
42
,
43
]
,
but this issue and possible consequences in the context of the present paper remain to be investigated.
